## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 68 page(s) - 68 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R4: Page has a descriptive title](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r4)
**Issue**: The `lang` attribute is either missing, empty, or only whitespace

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<html>...</html>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/html
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/html')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://mass.gov/doc/it-accessibility-services-contract-user-guide/download
- https://mass.gov/doc/2015-cost-trends-report-finding-drug-spending/download
- https://mass.gov/doc/2015-cost-trends-report-findings-emergency-department-and-apms/download
- https://mass.gov/doc/2015-cost-trends-report-preliminary-findings/download
- https://mass.gov/doc/c-l-14-request-for-findings-of-facts/download
- *...and 63 more page(s)*

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 11 page(s) - 11 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**Issue**: Target has insufficient size

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button class="ma__brand-banner-container" id="ma__brand-banner-button" aria-controls="ma__brand-banner-content" aria-expanded="false">...</button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/button[@id="ma__brand-banner-button"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser

3. Go to Console tab
